/tmp/linux/Documentation/security/keys/trusted-encrypted.rst:23: consumer of the Trusted Keys to determine if the trust source is sufficiently

/tmp/linux/Documentation/security/keys/trusted-encrypted.rst:62:          environment verified via Secure/Trusted boot process.
